- **patent-audit**: 检索证据链的结构化审计：权利要求要素分解、覆盖率台账（要素×数据源×分类号）、 对抗验证每个「要素↔对比文件」映射、独立复核逐条引文与定位、产出三态结论 （confirmed / needs_validation / rejected）与中立报告。 本技能审查已有工作区的证据质量与覆盖缺口，不产生新的检索结论，不预测授权； guidance 模式默认只给方法与局部核查，全面审计（full audit）必须显式授权才执行。 工具不可用/未运行时，回答必须以「⚠️ 未经验证工具校验」开头。 当用户要审计检索覆盖、复核证据链、对抗式验证对比文件映射、检查引文是否逐字可溯、 出具中立审计报告、找检索盲区时使用。 命中「全面审计」「audit」「审计检索链」「覆盖率」「对抗验证」「独立复核」 「证据链质量」「检索盲区」等说法时优先触发。 ([cyy1018abc-kyy/patent-research-skill](https://github.com/cyy1018abc-kyy/patent-research-skill/tree/HEAD/audit) · ★8)

- **patent-disclosure-builder**: 面向发明人的对话式专利交底书（技术交底书）生成助手。当发明人本人想把脑子里的技术方案整理成一份结构规范、内容完整的交底书，但不熟悉专利撰写规范时，应使用本技能。它以"填空式模板+对话逐项补全"的方式，一次只问一两个大白话问题，主动从专利视角深挖区别技术特征、有益效果、替代方案、上位概括等发明人最易忽略的关键点（软件/算法类还会强制做可专利客体自查）；还支持发明人直接提供现成材料（PPT/设计文档/论文/代码说明/评审纪要等），先消化预填、再只补问缺口，大幅省力；最终套用用户的标准 Word 模板产出可直接交给专利代理人的交底书（默认 .docx 输出）。触发场景包括："帮我写份交底书""我有个发明想申请专利，怎么整理材料""引导我把技术方案讲清楚""生成技术交底书""我不会写交底书，你问我吧""我有份 PPT/文档，帮我整理成交底书"等。区别于 patent-disclosure-review（审核已有交底书）和 patent-disclosure-writer（代理人撰写视角）。 ([CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub](https://github.com/CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub/tree/HEAD/skills/patent-disclosure-builder) · ★37)
- **patent-disclosure-exam**: 说明书充分公开审查（A26.3）：整体评估、问题识别（清楚性/完整性/能够实现性）、特殊领域审查、改进建议。用户要求审查说明书公开是否充分、A26.3 审查时使用。 ([xujian519/sati](https://github.com/xujian519/sati/tree/HEAD/skills/patent-disclosure-exam) · ★3)

- **pathway-gsea**: Gene Set Enrichment Analysis using clusterProfiler gseGO and gseKEGG. Use when analyzing ranked gene lists to find coordinated expression changes in gene sets without arbitrary significance cutoffs. Detects subtle but coordinated expression changes. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/pathway-analysis-gsea) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **pathway-hyperlink-classification-training**: Use when labeled pathway sets (positives from TomatoCyc, negatives from MetaCyc plus random sampling) must be classified from metabolite correlation network waveforms, or when reproducing NetAurHPD-style AUC benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-hyperlink-classification-training))
- **pathway-impact-plot-visualization**: Use when you have ORA enrichment results (adjusted Fisher's exact-test p-values on KEGG pathways) plus a significant metabolite list, and need to prioritize pathways by network topology impact rather than p-value rank alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-impact-plot-visualization))
- **pathway-importance-scoring**: Use when you have fitted a pathway-level multi-omics model (PathIntegrate MB-PLS/Single-View or DIABLO on ssPA-transformed data) and need ranked pathway importance statistics with permutation-tested significance or detection AUCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-importance-scoring))
- **pathway-inference-agent**: Use this agent when you need to create a pathway summary for a gene after completing the main gene review. This agent should be invoked specifically to generate GENE-pathway.md files that summarize the gene's role in biological pathways with proper citations and mermaid diagrams. ([ai4curation/ai-gene-review](https://github.com/ai4curation/ai-gene-review/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pathway-inference-agent) · ★25)
- **pathway-introduction-expert**: Generates comprehensive academic introductions for biological pathways, including signaling processes, markers, and inhibitors. Use when the user asks to introduce a pathway, molecule, or gene. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Evidence%20Insight/pathway-introduction-expert) · ★2.1k)
- **pathway-kegg-pathways**: KEGG pathway and module enrichment analysis using clusterProfiler enrichKEGG and enrichMKEGG. Use when identifying metabolic and signaling pathways over-represented in a gene list. Supports 4000+ organisms via KEGG online database. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/pathway-analysis-kegg-pathways) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **pathway-kernel-association-testing**: Use when you have paired genotype and phenotype data and want to test for association between a predefined genetic pathway (set of genes or variants) and a quantitative or binary trait, particularly when individual-variant tests lack power or when pathway-level aggregation is scientifically. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pathway-kernel-association-testing))
- **pathway-level-activity-scoring**: Use when you have a metabolite intensity matrix (samples × metabolites) with assigned annotations (peak IDs mapped to KEGG or ChEBI compound IDs), a metabolic pathway database, and need to rank pathways by activity level within experimental groups or comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pathway-level-activity-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **pathway-level-clustering**: Use when you have a PDS score matrix (0–1 pathway deregulation scores) restricted to Lilikoi-selected pathway features and want to check whether pathway-level profiles visually separate phenotype classes before or after classification modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-level-clustering))
- **pathway-level-feature-selection**: Use when you have a per-sample pathway score matrix (e.g., Lilikoi PDS matrix built by lilikoi.PDSfun) and need to pick the most significant pathway features before running a classification or prognosis model in Lilikoi v2.0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-level-feature-selection))
- **pathway-level-sample-stratification**: Use when you have per-sample metabolite abundance data and a metabolite-to-pathway association table, and need to assign each sample a dysregulation score for each pathway to enable downstream pathway-level classification, feature selection, or survival analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pathway-level-sample-stratification))
- **pathway-level-statistical-inference**: Use when when you have genotype data (variants grouped by pathway annotation) and quantitative or binary phenotype data, and you want to test whether a pathway as a whole is associated with the phenotype, rather than testing individual variants. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pathway-level-statistical-inference))
- **pathway-level-vs-molecular-level-signal-detection-benchmarking**: Use when you need to empirically justify a pathway-transformation step by comparing pathway-level versus molecular-level univariate detection power on injected-signal simulations spanning effect sizes 0–1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-level-vs-molecular-level-signal-detection-benchmarking))
- **pathway-loss-gain-rate-quantification**: Use when you need to stress-test the stability of an ORA-derived list of significantly enriched metabolomics pathways against compound annotation errors, e.g. measuring how many baseline pathways (p &lt;= 0.1) are lost or falsely gained at a given simulated misidentification rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-loss-gain-rate-quantification))

- **pathway-matching** ([aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform](https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform/tree/HEAD/super-agent/industry-packs/industry-pack-healthcare/scopes/clinical-decision-support/skills/pathway-matching) · ★27 · 2 copies)
- **pathway-metabolite-mapping**: Use when annotating m/z features from preprocessed organ-specific MSI data (e.g. AFADESI-MSI in positive/negative mode) and tracing which metabolic pathways are altered across multiple organs, such as liver, brain, and kidney. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-metabolite-mapping))
- **pathway-metabolite-mapping-integration**: Use when when you have metabolite-level summary statistics (p-values, log2 fold changes) from differential analysis and need to test whether specific metabolic pathways are significantly enriched in your dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pathway-metabolite-mapping-integration))
- **pathway-monitoring** ([aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform](https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform/tree/HEAD/super-agent/industry-packs/industry-pack-healthcare/scopes/clinical-decision-support/skills/pathway-monitoring) · ★27 · 2 copies)
- **pathway-network-construction**: Use when a metabolomics feature list must be mapped to pathway database identifiers (KEGG compound IDs) to enable pathway-based network construction or enrichment, and when you need to quantify how many features are actually annotatable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pathway-network-construction))
- **pathway-network-visualization**: Use when a pareg fit (or an enrichment result convertible via as_enrichplot_object) must be inspected for inter-pathway structure — clustered groups of related terms — rather than only a top-N term ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pathway-network-visualization))
